SELECT CASE [t].[Status]
    WHEN 0 THEN N'New'
    WHEN 1 THEN N'Assigned'
    WHEN 2 THEN N'InProgress'
    WHEN 3 THEN N'Resolved'
    WHEN 4 THEN N'Closed'
    ELSE CAST([t].[Status] AS nvarchar(max))
END AS [Status], COUNT(*) AS [Count]
FROM [Tickets] AS [t]
GROUP BY [t].[Status]

SELECT TOP(@p) [u].[FullName] AS [EmployeeName], COUNT(CASE
    WHEN [t].[Status] = 3 THEN 1
END) AS [ResolvedCount]
FROM [Tickets] AS [t]
LEFT JOIN [Users] AS [u] ON [t].[AssignedEmployeeId] = [u].[Id]
WHERE [t].[AssignedEmployeeId] IS NOT NULL
GROUP BY [t].[AssignedEmployeeId], [u].[FullName]
ORDER BY COUNT(CASE
    WHEN [t].[Status] = 3 THEN 1
END) DESC

SELECT CASE [t].[Status]
    WHEN 0 THEN N'New'
    WHEN 1 THEN N'Assigned'
    WHEN 2 THEN N'InProgress'
    WHEN 3 THEN N'Resolved'
    WHEN 4 THEN N'Closed'
    ELSE CAST([t].[Status] AS nvarchar(max))
END AS [Status], COUNT(*) AS [Count]
FROM [Tickets] AS [t]
GROUP BY [t].[Status]

SELECT TOP(@p) [u].[FullName] AS [EmployeeName], COUNT(CASE
    WHEN [t].[Status] = 3 THEN 1
END) AS [ResolvedCount]
FROM [Tickets] AS [t]

WHERE [t].[AssignedEmployeeId] IS NOT NULL

    WHEN [t].[Status] = 3 THEN 1
END) DESC

SELECT CASE [t].[Status]
    WHEN 0 THEN N'New'
    WHEN 1 THEN N'Assigned'
    WHEN 2 THEN N'InProgress'
    WHEN 3 THEN N'Resolved'
    WHEN 4 THEN N'Closed'

    OFFSET @p ROWS FETCH NEXT @p2 ROWS ONLY
) AS [t0]

LEFT JOIN [Users] AS [u0] ON [t0].[AssignedEmployeeId] = [u0].[Id]

SELECT TOP(@p) [u].[FullName] AS [EmployeeName], COUNT(CASE
    WHEN [t].[Status] = 3 THEN 1
END) AS [ResolvedCount]
FROM [Tickets] AS [t]
LEFT JOIN [Users] AS [u] ON [t].[AssignedEmployeeId] = [u].[Id]
WHERE [t].[AssignedEmployeeId] IS NOT NULL
GROUP BY [t].[AssignedEmployeeId], [u].[FullName]
ORDER BY COUNT(CASE
    WHEN [t].[Status] = 3 THEN 1
END) DESC

SELECT CASE [t].[Status]
    WHEN 0 THEN N'New'
    WHEN 1 THEN N'Assigned'
    WHEN 2 THEN N'InProgress'
    WHEN 3 THEN N'Resolved'
    WHEN 4 THEN N'Closed'
    ELSE CAST([t].[Status] AS nvarchar(max))
END AS [Status], COUNT(*) AS [Count]
FROM [Tickets] AS [t]
GROUP BY [t].[Status]

SELECT CASE [t].[Status]
    WHEN 0 THEN N'New'
    WHEN 1 THEN N'Assigned'
    WHEN 2 THEN N'InProgress'
    WHEN 3 THEN N'Resolved'
    WHEN 4 THEN N'Closed'
    ELSE CAST([t].[Status] AS nvarchar(max))
END AS [Status], COUNT(*) AS [Count]
FROM [Tickets] AS [t]

SELECT TOP(@p) [u].[FullName] AS [EmployeeName], COUNT(CASE
    WHEN [t].[Status] = 3 THEN 1
END) AS [ResolvedCount]
FROM [Tickets] AS [t]

WHERE [t].[AssignedEmployeeId] IS NOT NULL
GROUP BY [t].[AssignedEmployeeId], [u].[FullName]
ORDER BY COUNT(CASE
    WHEN [t].[Status] = 3 THEN 1
END) DESC

ORDER BY COUNT(CASE
    WHEN [t].[Status] = 3 THEN 1
END) DESC
